Title: Bengelius's Introduction to his Exposition of the Apocalypse, with his Preface to that Work and the greatest part of the Conclusion of it, and also his Marginal Notes on the Text, which are a Summary of the whole Exposition; translated from the High-Dutch by John Robertson, M.D.
Creator: Johann Albrecht Bengel (1687-1752); translated by John Robertson, M.D.
Publisher: London: sold by J. Ryall and R. Withy
Publication Date: 1757
